BUSINESS OVERVIEW AND PROSPECTS                                                業務回顧及展望
1.   BUSINESS OVERVIEW                                                         1.   業務總覽
     Artificial intelligence (AI) first demonstrated its productivity-              隨著人工智能在內容生成及軟件編程等
     enhancing value in digital applications such as content generation             數字化場景率先展現生產力價值，其
     and software development. Now, its application boundaries are                  應用邊界正加速由數字世界延伸至科
     rapidly extending from the digital world into scientific R&D and               學研發及真實物理世界。AI for Science
     the physical world. AI for Science (AI4S), or AI-enabled scientific            （AI4S, AI賦能科學創新）貫通科學發現
     innovation, connects scientific discovery with technological                   與技術創新；無論是全球科技產業領軍
     innovation. Leaders of the global technology industry and eminent              者，還是頂尖科研機構的學術領袖，均
     academics at leading research institutions alike regard AI4S as                將其視為人工智能下一階段最具變革潛
     one of the most transformative frontiers in the next stage of AI               力的前沿方向，有望重塑科研範式、拓
     development, with the potential to reshape the scientific research             展人類知識邊界，並開啟新一輪技術革
     paradigm, expand the frontiers of human knowledge, and                         命與產業躍遷。據行業研究預測，AI4S
     usher in a new wave of technological revolution and industrial                 市場空間有望突破1,400億美元，當前
     transformation. Industry research indicates that the AI4S market               滲透率仍處於極低水平；長期來看，隨
     is on track to exceed USD140 billion, with current penetration                 著AI4S能力向下游產業鏈及更廣泛的科
     still very low. Over the longer term, the AI4S end market has the              研場景延伸，有望進一步打開萬億美元
     potential to reach the trillion-USD level, as capabilities extend              級的終端市場空間。本集團認為，AI4S
     into downstream value chains and a broader range of scientific                 的核心在於構建貫通數字世界與物理世
     research scenarios. The Group believes that the essence of AI4S                界的智能基礎設施，形成覆蓋從科學推
     lies in building intelligent infrastructure that bridge the digital and        理到實驗驗證的全流程能力，推動人
     physical worlds, forming end-to-end capabilities spanning scientific           工智能由輔助科研的單點工具邁向「自
     reasoning through experimental validation, and advancing AI from               主科學發現」。在這一模式下，人工智
     isolated tools that assist scientific research toward autonomous               能不再僅根據指令完成局部任務，而是
     scientific discovery. Under this model, AI moves beyond executing              能夠在實際科研活動中自主承擔任務組
     individual tasks in response to instructions and is able, in actual            織、專業決策、實驗驗證及迭代優化等
     scientific research activities, to autonomously undertake a                    更廣泛的科研職能。
     broader range of research functions, including task orchestration,
     specialized decision-making, experimental validation and iterative
     optimization.

     On this basis, the Group has taken the lead in defining five levels of         據此，本集團率先提出將科學自主
     autonomous scientific discovery: L1 Tools (static tools), L2 Co-Pilots         發 現 劃 分 為 五 個 層 級 ： L1 Tools（ 靜
     (assisting humans), L3 Agents (independently completing parts                  態 工 具 ）、L2 Co-Pilots（ 輔 助 人 類 ）、
     of a workflow), L4 Domain-Specific Autonomous (full-workflow                   L3 Agents（ 獨 立 完 成 部 分 流 程 ）、L4
     autonomy within a specific domain) and L5 General Autonomous                   Domain-Specific Autonomous（ 領 域 全
     AI4S (autonomous AI-enabled scientific innovation across a                     流 程 自 主 ）及 L5 General Autonomous
     broad range of domains). This framework reflects the Group’s                   AI4S（泛領域自主AI賦能科學創新）。該
     fundamental view of autonomous scientific discovery: its essence               分級體系明確了本集團對科學自主發現
     lies not in the simple aggregation of models, robots or computing              的核心判斷：其本質並非模型、機器人
     resources, but in AI’s ability to autonomously undertake real-world            或算力資源的簡單疊加，而是人工智能
     scientific research tasks, independently make specialized decisions            自主承擔真實科研任務、獨立作出專業
     and continuously advance scientific discovery.                                 決策並持續推進科學發現的能力。

                                                                                                         公司簡介
                                                                                             Corporate Profile




      Extending autonomous scientific discovery from specific                          推動科學自主發現從特定領域及具體任
      domains and individual workflows to more complex                                 務流程向更複雜的科研任務和更廣泛的
      scientific research tasks and broader application scenarios                      應用場景延伸，仍面臨三方面關鍵制
      continues to face three key constraints. First, the supply of                    約：一是高質量科學數據供給不足，可
      high-quality scientific data remains insufficient, while reusable                複用評價標準及工程化基礎仍待完善；
      evaluation standards and engineering foundations require further                 二是人工智能生成的科研方案與真實實
      development. Second, research plans generated by AI have yet to                  驗執行之間尚未充分貫通；三是人工智
      be fully connected with real-world experimental execution. Third,                能在持續學習、自我進化、跨領域遷移
      AI’s capabilities in continuous learning, recursive improvement,                 及原創發現方面的能力仍需提升。為突
      cross-domain transfer and original discovery need to be further                  破上述制約並構建科學自主發現所需的
      enhanced. To address these constraints and build the end-to-end                  全鏈條能力，本集團持續推進研發體系
      capabilities required for autonomous scientific discovery, the Group             AI賦能及AI原生組織建設，將科學智能
      continues to embed AI across its R&D operations and advance                      體逐步嵌入研發、項目管理及組織協

         1 GENERAL INFORMATION                                                            1 一般資料
         XTALPI HOLDINGS LIMITED (the “Company”) was incorporated in the                  晶泰控股有限公司（「本公司」）於2017年4月28
         Cayman Islands on 28 April 2017 as an exempted company with limited              日在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司，註
         liabilities. The address of its registered office is PO Box 309, Ugland House,   冊辦事處的地址為PO Box 309, Ugland House,
         Grand Cayman KY1–1104, Cayman Islands.                                           Grand Cayman KY1-1104, Cayman Islands。

         The Company is an investment holding company. The Company and                    本公司為投資控股公司。本公司及其附屬公司
         its subsidiaries (collectively, the “Group”) are principally engaged in          （統稱「本集團」）主要從事提供基於創新型研究
         the provision of drug discovery solutions and AI for Science intelligent         平台的藥物發現解決方案及AI for Science智慧
         solutions based on its innovative research platform driven by artificial         解決方案，該平台由人工智能及機器人技術驅
         intelligence (“AI”) and robotics technologies that is driving intelligent and    動，推動生命科學及材料科學的智能及數字化
         digital innovations in life sciences and materials sciences. The Company’s       創新。本公司股份已自2024年6月13日起在香
         shares have been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong          港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。
         Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) since 13 June 2024.

         The condensed consolidated interim financial information is presented in         除非另有所示，否則簡明合併中期財務資料以
         thousands of unit of Renminbi (RMB’000), unless otherwise stated.                人民幣千元（人民幣千元）單位呈列。

         This condensed consolidated interim financial information for the six            截至2026年6月30日止六個月的簡明合併中期
         months ended 30 June 2026 is unaudited and has been reviewed by the              財務資料未經審核但已由本公司審核委員會及
         audit committee and external auditor of the Company. This condensed              外部核數師審閱。該簡明合併中期財務資料於

         This condensed consolidated interim financial information of the                 本集團截至2026年6月30日止六個月的簡明合

         Accounting Standard (“IAS”) 34 “Interim Financial Reporting” issued by           國際會計準則（「國際會計準則」）第34號「中期
         the International Accounting Standards Board (“IASB”).                           財務報告」編製。

         The Interim Financial Information does not include all the notes of the type     中期財務資料不包括年度財務報表中通常包含
         normally included in annual financial statements. Accordingly, this Interim      的所有類型附註。因此，本中期財務資料應與
         Financial Information should be read in conjunction with the consolidated        本集團截至2025年12月31日止年度根據國際
         financial statements of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2025,           會計準則理事會頒佈的國際財務報告準則會計
         which have been prepared in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards            準則編製的合併財務報表一併閱讀。
         issued by the IASB.

         The preparation of the Interim Financial Information requires management         編製中期財務資料需要管理層作出判斷、估計
         to make judgments, estimates and assumptions that affect the application         及假設，而有關判斷、估計及假設會影響會計
         of accounting policies and the reported amounts of assets and liabilities,       政策的應用，以及資產、負債、收入及開支的
         income and expenses. Actual results may differ from these estimates. In          呈報金額。實際結果可能與該等估計有所不
         preparing this Interim Financial Information, the significant judgments          同。於編製本中期財務資料時，管理層於應用
         made by management in applying the Group’s accounting policies and               本集團會計政策過程中作出的重大判斷，以及
         the key sources of estimation uncertainty are consistent with those applied      估計不確定因素的主要來源，均與截至2025
         in the consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31 December          年12月31日止年度之合併財務報表所採用者

         A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the liability is   當金融負債項下的責任獲履行或註銷或屆滿時
         discharged or cancelled or expires.                                                終止確認金融負債。

         When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the same          若現有金融負債由相同放款人按完全不同之條
         lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an existing liability     款提供另一項負債取代，或現有負債之條款作
         are substantially modified, such an exchange or modification is treated as         出重大修訂，則上述取代或修訂視為終止確認
         a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of a new liability,    原有負債及確認新負債，而相關賬面值之差額
         and the difference between the respective carrying amounts is recognised           在損益表確認。
         in the statement of profit or loss.

         exchange of a fixed amount of cash or another financial asset for a fixed          方式結算的轉換期權為權益工具。與發行可轉
         number of the Company’s own equity instruments is an equity instrument.            換債券有關之交易成本，乃按金融負債及權益

the market risk (including foreign exchange risk, cash flow and fair value        風險（包括外匯風險、現金流量及公允價值利
interest rate risk and price risk), credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s   率風險及價格風險）、信貸風險及流動資金風
overall risk management programme focuses on the unpredictability of              險。本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市
financial markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse effects on the          場的難預測性，旨在將對本集團財務表現的潛
Group’s financial performance.                                                    在不利影響減至最低。

3.2 Fair value estimation                                                         3.2 公允價值估計

fair value hierarchy as follows:                                                  價值層級內分為以下三個層級：

• Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded in active markets       • 第一層級：於活躍市場買賣的金融工具（例
(such as publicly traded derivatives, and equity securities) is based on          如公開買賣的衍生工具及股本證券）的公允價
quoted market prices at the end of the reporting period. The quoted               值是按報告期末市場報價而定。本集團所持金
market price used for financial assets held by the Group is the current bid       融資產使用的市場報價為當前買入價。該等工
price. These instruments are included in level 1.                                 具列入第一層級。

• Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that are not traded in an       • 第二層級：並非於活躍市場買賣的金融工具
active market (for example, over-the-counter derivatives) is determined           （例如場外衍生工具）的公允價值乃利用估值技
using valuation techniques which maximise the use of observable                   術釐定，該等技術盡量利用可觀察市場數據並
market data and rely as little as possible on entity-specific estimates. If all   盡可能減少對實體特定估計的倚賴。倘工具的
significant inputs required to fair value an instrument are observable, the       公允價值所需的所有重大輸入數據為可觀察數
instrument is included in level 2.                                                據，則該工具列入第二層級。

• Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs is not based on               • 第三層級：倘一項或多項重大輸入數據並非

case for unlisted equity securities.                                              層級。此亦適用於非上市股本證券。

25 RELATED-PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                    25 關聯方交易
Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly or   若一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或在作
indirectly, to control the other party or exercise significant influence over    出財務及經營決策方面對另一方發揮重大影
the other party in making financial and operational decisions. Parties are       響，即視為有關聯。倘所涉各方受共同控制，
also considered to be related if they are subjected to common control.           則亦視為相互關聯。本集團主要管理人員及彼
Members of key management and their close family members of the                  等近親家庭成員亦視為關聯方。
Group are also considered as related parties.

The following significant transactions were carried out between the Group        截至2026年6月30日止期間，本集團與其關聯
and its related parties during the period ended 30 June 2026. In the             方開展以下重大交易。本公司董事認為，關聯
opinion of the directors of the Company, the related party transactions          方交易於日常業務過程中按本集團與各關聯方
were carried out in the normal course of business and at terms negotiated        磋商的條款進行。
between the Group and the respective related parties.
